Milei advocates [minimal government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Night-watchman_state), focusing on administering justice and ensuring security,[^cite_note-Il_Post_2023-6] with a philosophy rooted in [life, liberty, and property](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Life,_Liberty_and_the_pursuit_of_Happiness#Lockean_roots_hypothesis), and [free market](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_market) principles. He criticizes [socialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Socialism) and [communism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communism),[^cite_note-Cronista_2019-360] advocating [economic liberalization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economic_liberalization) and restructuring of government ministries.[^cite_note-361] He opposes Argentina's Central Bank and current taxation policies.[^cite_note-Centenera_&_Criales_2023-362][^cite_note-363]

Economically, Milei is influenced by the [Austrian school](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Austrian_school_of_economics), and admires former President [Carlos Menem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carlos_Menem)'s policies.[^cite_note-Fiore_Viani_2021-364] He supports [capitalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capitalism), viewing socialism as embodying envy and coercion.[^cite_note-Cronista_2019-360] Milei proposes reducing government ministries and addressing economic challenges through spending cuts and fiscal reforms, criticizing previous administrations for excessive spending.[^cite_note-Viriglio_2023-9][^cite_note-365] He has praised the monetary policies of former British prime minister [Margaret Thatcher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margaret_Thatcher), and called her "a great leader".[^cite_note-366][^cite_note-367][^cite_note-368]

Milei opposes [abortion](765.md)[^cite_note-369] and [euthanasia](9587.md),[^cite_note-370] and supports [privatization](24661.md) in education and health care. He opposes [mandatory vaccination](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vaccination_policy), and supports [drug legalization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drug_liberalization) and the [legalization of prostitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prostitution_law#Regulated_prostitution).[^cite_note-Rossi_2021-371][^cite_note-El_Cronista_2023-372] As a supporter of the [right to keep and bear arms](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Right_to_keep_and_bear_arms), Milei advocates for the deregulation of firearm ownership, and proposes [immigration restrictions](6491626.md) for criminals.[^cite_note-Il_Post_2023-6][^cite_note-373] In foreign policy, Milei criticizes the [IMF](15251.md),[^cite_note-Nugent_2023-374] opposes [trade unions](17626.md),[^cite_note-Infonews.com_2023-375] aligns with [anti-socialist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Criticism_of_socialism) figures of the [Americas](29833.md) like [Donald Trump](4848272.md) and [Jair Bolsonaro](46603496.md),[^cite_note-376][^cite_note-Gosman_2023-377] and prioritizes alliances with the United States and Israel.[^cite_note-378][^cite_note-379] Milei has disputed the estimated death toll of the Dirty War, and removed funding for special prosecutors, claiming it violates the country's constitution.[^cite_note-380] He had been critical of relations with China because of its communist ideology during his presidential campaign, but he came to see China as a business partner in the first year of his presidency.[^cite_note-381][^cite_note-382] He supports Ukraine against Russia in the latter's [ongoing invasion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine).[^cite_note-383] He opposes the [transgender rights movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transgender_rights_movement), and what he calls a "cult of [gender ideology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-gender_movement)". He repealed the decree that established a job quota for transgender people in the public sector.[^cite_note-384][^cite_note-385][^cite_note-386]

Milei advocates for dialogue with the [United Kingdom](31717.md) over the [Falkland Islands sovereignty dispute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falkland_Islands_sovereignty_dispute).[^cite_note-Alonso_2023-58] In May 2024, he indicated, for the time being, his general acceptance of the [Falkland Islands](7550182.md)' status as a [British Overseas Territory](207114.md), accepting that it could take decades for Argentina to regain control of them. Though asserting that Argentina would not "relinquish \[our] sovereignty" over the islands, Milei said he would not "seek conflict with the United Kingdom", preferring to resolve the dispute "within the framework of peace".[^cite_note-decades_2024_05_06_bbc-387]

Javier Milei has adopted a strongly pro-Israel foreign policy stance since taking office. According to Reuters, his administration has expressed intentions to strengthen diplomatic relations with Israel, and re-orient Argentina's foreign policy closer to Israeli positions. Milei has considered relocating Argentina's embassy from Tel Aviv to Jerusalem, a move that would align Argentina with a small number of countries recognizing Jerusalem as Israel's capital. He is one of the most pro-Israel leaders in Latin America, particularly following his election and subsequent diplomatic engagements with Israeli officials.[^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389][^cite_note-390]

## Public image

Main article: [Public image of Javier Milei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_image_of_Javier_Milei)

Milei has cultivated a complex and controversial public image marked by a blend of [populist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Populism), right-wing libertarian, and [conservative](6675.md) ideologies. Known for his [ultra-liberal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ultraliberalism#Market_liberalism) economic views and [right-wing populist](8354382.md) rhetoric, Milei's political stance has been subject to various interpretations by international media and political commentators.[^cite_note-Molina_2023-57][^cite_note-Alonso_2023-58] His rise to prominence during the 2023 presidential campaign, fueled by his primary win, sparked widespread attention,[^cite_note-Reuters_2023-103][^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_2023-104][^cite_note-Goñi_2023-105] as did his [central bank](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_bank) abolition and [dollarization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Currency_substitution) proposals.[^cite_note-Sivak_2023-391]



Milei with Hungarian Prime Minister [Viktor Orbán](750582.md)

Milei is known for his flamboyant personality, distinctive personal style, and strong media presence, which sometimes causes controversy, and his embrace of [conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theory),[^cite_note-392] including the [Cultural Marxism conspiracy theory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cultural_Marxism_conspiracy_theory).[^cite_note-393] He has also called the idea of [climate change](5042951.md) as an anthropogenic phenomenon "a socialist lie" and said that concerns about it are nothing more than "deceptions promoted by the neo-Marxists",[^cite_note-394][^cite_note-Gosman_2023-377] as are those related to the [attempts to overturn the 2020 United States presidential election](65905202.md).[^cite_note-395] Echoing [Donald Trump](4848272.md), he also claimed electoral fraud ahead of the 2023 presidential runoff.[^cite_note-396][^cite_note-397] Like other right-wing populists, his rhetoric focuses on opposing what he calls "the political caste".[^cite_note-Dadouch_2023-398] Milei's party was criticized for including among his candidates apologists for the [National Reorganization Process](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Reorganization_Process).[^cite_note-iProfesional_2023-77] During his political career, Milei has also been involved in several investigations and has been accused of having a violent attitude toward journalists and critics, as well as of misogynistic behavior, including toward women in journalism.[^cite_note-BBC_News_Mundo_2023-42]

Milei is a [cosplayer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cosplay) with a [superhero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Superhero) persona called "General AnCap".[^cite_note-apnews-399] He also champions [free love](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_love).[^cite_note-400][^cite_note-401][^cite_note-402] In addition to being nicknamed *el Peluca* ("The Wig") for his eccentric hair,[^cite_note-403][^cite_note-404][^cite_note-405] which has been compared to that of Trump and fellow right-wing populists [Boris Johnson](19065069.md) and [Geert Wilders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geert_Wilders),[^cite_note-406] Milei is known as *el Loco* ("The Madman").[^cite_note-González_2023-407] News outlets have likened him to both Trump and [Wolverine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wolverine_\(character\)).[^cite_note-408] His admirers also call him "The Lion" because of the lyrics of the song "Panic Show" by rock band [La Renga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Renga), which he sings in his public acts, coupled with his looks (in particular, his long hair).[^cite_note-apnews-399][^cite_note-409] *[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\))* named him one of the [100 most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100) in 2024 and 2025.[^cite_note-auto3-410][^cite_note-auto1-411]



Milei with [Elon Musk](909036.md) holding a chainsaw; the chainsaw has become a popular symbol associated with Milei.

The [chainsaw](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chainsaw) has become an enduring and popular symbol associated with Milei[^cite_note-412] (he has been called the "chainsaw candidate"[^cite_note-413]), specifically symbolizing his "cutting" of regulations, bureaucracy, and [red tape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_tape) in Argentina. Some commentators have called Milei's economic policy of cutting regulations "chainsaw economics".[^cite_note-414] Milei's supporters often carry chainsaws at rallies, symbolizing "his promise to cut down the size of the state".[^cite_note-415] Milei is also well known for his phrase "[¡Viva la libertad, carajo!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C2%A1Viva_la_libertad,_carajo!)", which is uttered most especially at the end of various speeches given by him.[^cite_note-416]

### Other

Milei was awarded the [2025 Genesis Prize](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Genesis_Prize) in recognition of his support of Israel.[^cite_note-421] He was also awarded an honorary doctorate from [Bar-Ilan University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bar-Ilan_University) in 2026.[^cite_note-422] *[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\))* named him one of the [100 most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100) in 2024 and 2025.[^cite_note-auto3-410][^cite_note-auto1-411]

## Personal life



Milei (center) praying at [the grave](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohel_\(Chabad-Lubavitch\)) of [Menachem Mendel Schneerson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Mendel_Schneerson) on 27 November 2023

Milei is unmarried, and, while he was a presidential candidate, said that, if elected, he would have [his sister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karina_Milei) take the role of [First Lady of Argentina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Lady_of_Argentina).[^cite_note-Dadouch_2023-398] However, in December 2023, after winning the presidency, he announced that his government would not have a first lady.[^cite_note-\:3-423] He called the role "anachronistic", and declared that this decision was taken after discussions with both his sister and his girlfriend, actress [Fátima Flórez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%A1tima_Fl%C3%B3rez).[^cite_note-\:3-423] Milei originally announced he was dating Fátima Florez in August 2023.[^cite_note-424] Previously, he dated singer [Daniela Mori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniela_Mori?action=edit\&redlink=1).[^cite_note-425]

While raised [Catholic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic),[^cite_note-426][^cite_note-427][^cite_note-Gillespie_&_Tobias_2023-428] Milei had been critical of the [Catholic Church](606848.md) under [Pope Francis](1687680.md),[^cite_note-429][^cite_note-430] and his disparaging comments about Francis attracted criticism from Catholics.[^cite_note-431][^cite_note-432] Conversely, Milei would later embrace Francis after meeting him, stating in a [Retequattro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rete_4) interview, "I had to reconsider some positions, and starting from that moment, we began to build a positive relationship."[^cite_note-433][^cite_note-434] Milei also reads the [Torah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Torah) daily, and has visited [the grave](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohel_\(Chabad-Lubavitch\)) of Orthodox rabbi [Menachem Mendel Schneerson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Menachem_Mendel_Schneerson).[^cite_note-435][^cite_note-436][^cite_note-437] Before November 2023, Milei said he had contemplated [converting to Judaism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conversion_to_Judaism), but that observing the [Jewish Sabbath](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Sabbath) could pose challenges if he became president.[^cite_note-Sivak_2023-391][^cite_note-438][^cite_note-439] Upon being elected president, it was reported that Milei intended to convert to Judaism.[^cite_note-Gillespie_&_Tobias_2023-428] Milei has confessed that he professes a "fanaticism" for [Judaism](15624.md).[^cite_note-\:2-18]

Milei owns five cloned [English Mastiffs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_Mastiff), their progenitor being [Conan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conan_\(Javier_Milei's_dog\)), who died in 2017 after suffering from [spinal cancer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spinal_cancer).[^cite_note-iProfesional_2023-77][^cite_note-González_2023-407][^cite_note-El_Doce_2023-440] He considers Conan his son (a form of [pet humanization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pet_humanization)), and has named four of Conan's six [clones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cloning), including one named after the original and another named Angelito,[^cite_note-441] Milton (in honor of [Milton Friedman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Milton_Friedman)), Murray (in honor of [Murray Rothbard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murray_Rothbard)), Robert, and Lucas (both named after [Robert Lucas, Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Lucas_Jr.)).[^cite_note-442][^cite_note-443] Milei said that he cloned Conan because he understands cloning as "a way of approaching eternity".[^cite_note-El_Doce_2023-440] To do this, he went to a clinic in the United States. The process cost him about \$50,000.[^cite_note-El_Doce_2023-440] He has called his dogs his "four-legged children", and thanked them after his electoral win.[^cite_note-Viriglio_2023-9][^cite_note-444] Milei commented that one day, there was a fire at his building, while he was watching TV, which he realized because Conan made for the balcony. He escaped with him, using the emergency ladder, and took him to the vet, who gave oxygen to Milei because he was developing symptoms of [cyanosis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyanosis).[^cite_note-445]

Milei is an avid fan of [association football](10568.md). Albeit having played for [Chacarita Juniors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chacarita_Juniors) in his early adulthood, he has stated to be a supporter of [Boca Juniors](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boca_Juniors); in a 2024 interview with [Radio Mitre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radio_Mitre), Milei said, "I am a member and fan of Boca. If investment groups come and put a fortune for us to win all the time, where do I sign?"[^cite_note-446] However, he has expressed criticism of the presidency of [Juan Román Riquelme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juan_Rom%C3%A1n_Riquelme) at the club since 2023, stating that his "management at Boca is a disaster", and referred to Riquelme as "a [Kirchnerist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kirchnerism) running the club".[^cite_note-447] Milei is a huge fan of the English rock band [The Rolling Stones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Rolling_Stones).[^cite_note-448]

In December 2024, while holding the office of Head of State, Milei applied for, and was granted, [Italian citizenship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_nationality_law) via *[jus sanguinis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jus_sanguinis)*, under the government of Prime Minister [Giorgia Meloni](29228976.md).[^cite_note-449][^cite_note-450]
